([lapanti/lavanti.fi](https://github.com/lapanti/lavanti.fi/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/write))
- **write**: Apply the house documentation tone to any written deliverable. Use when writing, editing, or reviewing markdown documents, READMEs, Word documents, PDFs, HTML pages, reports, or technical documentation of any kind. Does not cover blog posts for lekman.com (the lekman-blog skill owns that voice) or conversational replies. ([lekman/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/lekman/ai-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/tone/skills/write))
